# Plan

Approved 2026-10-10. Rewritten 2026-10-12 with everything found so far (read this first after a context reset).

Goal: The Sims 3 (Steam 1.67, all EPs/SPs) with **less in-game stutter** first (stuck Sims, routing, sim-thread work; load times are fine), then fewer crashes (Error 12 / out of address space) and room for heavy CC. Rodney wants every avenue tried. Workflow: online prior art → reverse engineering → rank big wins → build, one change at a time, measured with the harness.

**Long-term goal: offload the sim thread (Rodney, 2026-10-14).** Move everything that can safely leave the game's single busy core (the sim thread) to other cores, so longer saves, more objects and more Sims scale instead of hitting that one core's limit. This needs a major architecture redesign and is a multi-step, long-term track (roadmap: docs/23 #17). Acceptance rule for offload changes: a change that moves measurable work off the sim thread is accepted even with no speed gain today, or a loss of up to ~2% in sim iterations/s, as long as it is stable (no crashes, no new stuck Sims, same results). Measure it as sim-thread ms/s moved off (route ms/s, stage ms/s), not only iterations/s. Losses over ~2% need a plan to win them back.

**Launch readiness before any promotion (Rodney 2026-10-29).** No MTS/Nexus/Reddit/forum posts or SEO until: (1) the fixes land with undeniable measured gains (freeze counts, worst hitch, sim ms/s, memory headroom, before/after on clean saves and a heavy-CC setup); (2) the answers are public: compatibility matrix + conflict switches, FAQ (save safety, what each switch changes, NRaas/S3SS/S3PP overlap, antivirus false positive, how we measure), method page with reproducible numbers. Effort goes to fixes, measurements and those answer docs first.

Monohost 3.30 ns a load through the thunks [measured]; model [inferred]: BP ~0.7, IP ~1.0 ms/s. (2) Queue `tools/harness/queues/satb-act.json` (Satb=2 Mode R, LazyGate=1, WindowWaitUs=0, smoke + BP/IP x 2 clean runs, A1-A3 0), (satb-s0b: BP PASS, IP borderline; the 0.36.136 fixes target both causes). (3) Env apply (`autoffenvapply`, off): requests take the Sims whose update is due next (tick of last update + period, >= half passed), the worker returns the scan's Sim/object sequence + room info, the sim applies at the Sim's own update (`EnvApply` = the game's method with the scan replaced) when lot, room, inside/outside and position match; ready store closes the Satb race on the worker's arrays. (4) Bcast: candidates from `LotManager.Actors` + Sim position rows (range query off the cut), apply (`autoffbcastapply`, off) rebuilds with live LOS / could-see when the answer is <= 15 ticks old; `autoffnoref` skips the cut reference for timing. Model [inferred]: env ~7-8, bcast ~9-10 ms/s x apply share. Counters 481-490. **Next:** park asi `game-copy/ts3native.asi.satb3` + `game-copy/ts3fixes.package.satb3`; after satb-s0b PASS: `node tools/harness/queue.ts tools/harness/queues/satb-act.json` -> `node tools/harness/mono-cost-report.ts --tag satb-act`; after satb-act and offload3-moder PASS: `node tools/harness/queue.ts tools/harness/queues/offload3-act.json`.

**Mono cut cost: tier by what the player can notice (Rodney 2026-10-29).** Snapshot work and worker answers are tiered: tier 1 (fresh every cut) = visible Sims, Sims near visible Sims, Sims near household Sims even when unseen; tier 2 (throttled, ~1-2 s) = far unseen Sims: their rows refresh and their autonomy is served less often, since a >1 s delay is not noticeable and they still act later. Reuse ts3fixes' unseen rule (UfWhy) and ts3native's viewer positions; a tier-2 row past its bound makes the worker abort (game does it), never a wrong answer. Built into mono-cost8 (with change-only refresh) or as a second switch.

**Worker window options 2026-10-30 (docs/206, static, ledger §398): build copy on first write; native span and copied heap rejected.** (1) Native span inside the iteration (`0xD81A41`..`0xD81CF5`: SACS pass, services, pre-locomotion, locomotion, animation) is BP ~3.3 / IP ~2.2 / SV ~1.4 ms a tick [inferred, v0.2.4 sim budget] and calls C# synchronously: route events 57/58 via `World_FireEvent` -> `LotManager.OnRoutingEventHandler` (bool return, cannot defer) and every SACS event push `0x742710` -> `EventQueue.OnNativeEvent` (refutes docs/22's "C# drains later") [verified]; reject. (2a) Full copy of the read set at the cut: 2-6 ms per cut [inferred]; reject. (2b) **Copy on first write**: from the cut to the worker's end the sim logs old values before every managed store (35 store opcodes / 24 mint handlers + array/Interlocked internal calls + IC byref ranges), the worker's 42 loads / 29 handlers + 13 superops check the log; handlers swapped in only while a job runs; window ~26-32 ms, overruns ~0, sim cost ~1-2.5 ms/s at Sample=1 [inferred]; exact if every post-cut writer logs (Mode R + heap-image audit check it); ~15-24 h + 3-5 h audit, 6-10 jobs; first time two threads interpret together in game (docs/131 open races). (3) Copied heap / COW: same-address views need a second process (clone per cut tens of ms + COW faults), page-protect COW ~1.5-30 ms per job, write watch belongs to the incremental GC and only finds conflicts; reject (docs/123 image stays the audit tool). Read set: `tools/worker-read-set.ts` -> `docs/data/worker-read-set.csv` (pilot union 510 fields, 275 mutable on 109 types + statics; CHA bound 8,637 / 3,563). **Next:** S0 store-rate probe (counting-only store handlers from cut to worker end, `satbStores`/`satbWords`/`satbPages`, 1-2 h, 2 jobs on the next mono-cost queue; stop if > ~5 ms/s), then `[AutShadow] Satb=1`, then Mode R with the sim running (results consumed at the next sim pump), then docs/124 S2-S6.

**Compatibility track (Rodney 2026-10-29, side work next to Mono + throttling; answers "not checked against other mods" online):**
1. `tools/compat-scan.ts` (~1-2 h): every method/type our ts3fixes hooks patch and every ts3native hook address; against a Mods folder: mods that replace or override the same methods/types (S3SA assemblies, MVIDs), overlapping resource/tuning IDs, other ASIs on our addresses (`hook-sites.ts`). Player-runnable, `--self-check`, output pasted into reports. Builds on `offload-override-scan.ts`, `hook-sites.ts`, docs/128, docs/132. **Built 2026-10-29** (`node tools/compat-scan.ts [<folder>...] [--json] [--matrix docs/data/compat-matrix.csv]`, static, ~3 s): 69 patched game methods resolved (14 run-time Patch calls not resolvable statically; other mods' types ts3fixes looks up by name count as overlap), 699 ts3native hook addresses listed. First run (compat-mods + installed Packages, 176 files): overlap 6 = simler90 core x2 (replaces Sims3GameplaySystems: most features), NRaas ErrorTrap (replaces ScriptCore: cachedreflection, fastreftable, RouteBackoff; NRaas.Common by name), NRaas Overwatch (by name: overwatchspread/notice/probe, errortrapspread), Store Updates 021-040 / 041-051 (override `ScriptObject.Destroy`: MemSuspects, diagnostic); unknown 9 (.rar: unpack needed); 161 no overlap. Not covered: ITUN/tuning-level overlap beyond shared resource keys, other runtime patchers (MonoPatcher) and ASIs (unknown).
2. Download every major routing mod (NRaas GoHere, Traffic, StoryProgression, Register, Overwatch, ErrorTrap, Retuner; OhRudi packs, TSS ToddlerRoutingFix, Nikel23 slopes, houseboat/ocean fixes, Smooth Patch, simler90 core, Arsil, S3PP, S3SS, Apex) into `game-copy/compat-mods/` (never committed; record URL + version + license in docs/24). Static pass per mod: what it changes in routing, overlap with our hooks, verdict.
3. Stand-down rule: where our behaviour is better, the other mod's overlapping feature stands down; where theirs has better custom logic, ours stands down (our switch off when their assembly/marker is present, logged, like Apex's ApexStandDown). Their feature is turned off only by telling players how (compat page, compat-scan output, release notes: which setting in their mod to change); we never change their ini/tunables/settings ourselves and never patch their code (NRaas/MTS code has no license; [[prefer-own-fixes-over-s3pp]] pattern). "Better" = measured (repro or ms/s), not assumed.
4. Top 100 non-routing mods (MTS most downloaded, Nexus, NRaas suite, community must-have lists): compatibility matrix `docs/data/compat-matrix.csv` (mod, version, kind script/tuning/ASI/CC, overlap with us, verdict compatible / compatible with switch X off / conflict / untested, evidence) via compat-scan + static read; top script mods get an in-game smoke with our release build.
5. Publish: compatibility page (tested-with list from our test install + the matrix, known conflicts, switch per conflict), linked from README and release notes; release check keeps the common mod set installed.
Downloads: public pages directly; MTS downloads that need login only through Rodney's Chrome (MTS tooling rules: on demand, human pacing). Estimate: steps 1-3 ~1 day wall clock with agents; step 4 ~1-2 days (mostly agents, static).

**Dead references + age-up outfits 2026-10-30 (issues #91, #100; docs/205, built, not run):** `ageupkeepoutfits` (#100, `EaFixes.cs`): `SimBuilderTask.FinishOutfitSwap` skipped when the build `Failed` or made no outfit (EA wipes every outfit after a failed or cancelled age-up). `deadrefguards` (#91): `Relationship.Get(d)` adds no key for an invalid `d`, `IsAgingStateValid` false for one. `deadrefsweep` (#91, changes save data): EA holders of disposed descriptions / destroyed Sims cleaned hourly in ~2 ms slices (relationships, aging states and alarms, react times, coworkers, chess, topics, fish, Sim fields, passengers, horseshoe) plus a cheap pre-save pass; never at load (stands down while any active-household member is invalid). All off; counters 391-410; self-check `tools/selftest/deadrefs`; repros `bp-ageup-outfits-check`, `bp-deadrefs-check`, `bp-deadrefs-saves`; queue `tools/harness/queues/deadrefs-ageup.json` (correctness + save matrix); reply drafts `docs/data/upstream/issue-91-reply.md`, `issue-100-reply.md` (not posted).

**Career uniform backup 2026-10-31 (issue #101; docs/502, verified 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP and BigSave-LR):** `uniformbackup` (`UniformBackup.cs`, off by default): `Occupation.ApplyOccupationUniform` uncaches the shared static `backupOutfit` at every later re-uniform and never clears it, so a new Career outfit that is handed the freed key loses its resource and does not load after a reload. With the switch on, the backup is cleared right after EA's one uncache. Counter 2002, repro `career-uniform-backup-check`, self-test `tools/selftest/uniformbackup`, ledger §416.

**Jazz parameter 2026-11-01 (issue #166 build 21; docs/511, verified 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `jazzparam` (`JazzParam.cs`, off by default): `Sim.UpdateJazzParameter` reads the posture's state machine without checking for a posture, so a Sim with none throws `NullReferenceException` out of `BuffEnjoyingMusic`'s add or remove. The hook catches that one exception. Counters 2035-2036, ledger §909, repro `jazz-param-check` (needs the original core mod).

**Buff update walk 2026-11-01 (issue #166 build 20; docs/510, verified 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `buffupdate` (`BuffUpdate.cs`, off by default): `BuffManager.UpdateBuffs` walks the live buff table and calls each timed buff's `OnTimeOutUpdated` inside the walk, so a buff added there (the suntan's `kBronzed` listeners) makes the walk throw out of sync, and nothing above catches it: under ErrorTrap the Sim is reset (seen live), without it its update task ends [inferred]. The hook catches that one exception. Counters 2033-2034, ledger §908, repro `buff-update-check` (needs the original core mod).

**Car drive with no routing 2026-11-01 (issue #166 build 33; docs/513, verified 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `carnoroute` (`CarNoRoute.cs`, off by default): `CarNpcManager.DriveFromPointToMatrix` uses the car's `CarRoutingComponent` with no check, so a destroyed car throws with the service Sim in the driver's seat. The hook returns false first, as for a failed plan; both callers handle that. Counters 2039-2040, ledger §911, repro `car-no-route-check` (needs the original core mod).

**Conversation turn 2026-11-01 (issue #166 build 19; docs/509, built, self-tested, repro PASS 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `convturn` (`ConvTurn.cs`, off by default): a conversation turn passes only when the Sim on turn picks a social, and only local Sims score socials, so a turn held by an off-screen townie never moves. A hook before `SocialComponent.ScoreSocialsOnSims()` gives a stuck turn (holder gone, not local, busy with a non-social, or silent 15 sim minutes) to the asking local Sim. Counters 2031-2032, ledger §907, repro `conversation-turn-check`.

**Deferred age-up abort 2026-11-01 (issue #166 build 32; docs/512, verified 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `ageupabort` (`AgeUpAbort.cs`, off by default): `AgingState.PreInstantiateSim` waits for a deferred age-up's outfit build; an abort during the wait nulls the task, the wait throws and `SimDescription.Instantiate` creates no Sim. The hook catches that one exception; the Sim is created with its current outfit and the age-up stays deferred. Counters 2037-2038, ledger §910, repro `age-up-abort-check` (needs the original core mod).

**Outfit waits 2026-10-31 (issue #115; docs/224, built, not run):** `outfitwaitpoll` (`OutfitWait.cs`, off by default): a `Sim.SwitchOutfitHelper.Wait` whose swap was aborted loops on `Simulator.Sleep(1)` for the rest of the session (the magical-makeover alarm in #115, 3.0 ms per sim-minute on the day-406 save). With the switch on, a wait not ready after 60 sim-minutes reads `mReady` once per sim-minute; it still returns at the first ready, so outfits and timing stay EA's to the sim-minute. Counters 680-682, repro `outfit-wait-stale-check`, self-test `tools/selftest/outfitwait`, ledger §414. Next: LR in-load `--ab outfitwaitpoll`.
**Bake sale seller 2026-10-31 (issue #102; docs/225, built, not run in this form):** `bakesaleseller` (`BakeSale.cs`, off by default): a bake sale table keeps a seller who was deleted while tending, so every buyer throws and an NPC table's clean-up handler throws and never removes the table. With the switch on, a deleted seller reads as none and an orphaned NPC table gets EA's clean-up alarm back at load. Counters 610-611, repro `bake-sale-dead-seller-check`, self-test `tools/selftest/bakesale`, ledger §415.
**Menu Maven recipe target 2026-11-01 (docs/501, built, self-tested, repro passed once):** `menumaven` (`MenuMaven.cs`, off by default): `Cooking.IsMenuMaven` decides the Menu Maven challenge and the Culinary Librarian wish with the tuned 28, never raised for the expansions, store or recipe mods; on, the Sim must know every recipe of the install that `Recipe.Learn` can teach (64 on a full install), counted live. Counters 2000-2001 (contributor range), ledger §900, repro `menumaven-all-recipes-check`, queue `menu-maven.json`.
**Null service 2026-11-01 (docs/504, verified in game on BP and LR):** `nullservice` (off by default): `Services.sAllServices` is saved as it is, so a saved service whose type no longer loads comes back as a null element and every EA loop over the list throws on it (object load via `ResortStaffedObjectComponent.GetService`, `PostWorldLoadStartup`, `Services.Simulate`, which would end the services task); the `AllServices` getter drops nulls and `PostWorldLoadStartup` reads the getter first; counter 2014, ledger §903, repro `null-service-check` (queue `null-service.json`). Seen live as 7-8 load-time NullReferenceExceptions on one custom bar object per lot (NRaas Overwatch removes the null only later in the load).
**Rabbit hole door 2026-11-01 (docs/505, verified in game on BP):** `rabbitholedoor` (off by default): `RabbitHole.AnimateDoorOpen` / `AnimateDoorClose` ask the rabbit hole state machine for `Open` / `Closed` of actor `Door<n>`; a door the animation cannot open throws `SacsErrorException`, which ends the Sim's entry (seen at Public School 67 on Baseline-BP, door 2); the hooks skip that error after EA's code ran, so the counts stay paired; counter 2015, ledger §904, repro `rabbit-hole-door-check` (queue `rabbit-hole-door.json`). Seen live twice (2026-10-31, 2026-11-01) as an ErrorTrap report for a teen entering school (NRaas Careers path).

**NPC driver pool 2026-11-01 (issue #166 build 37; docs/514, repro PASS 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `npcdriverpool` (off by default): `NpcDriversManager.CreateNpcDriver` hands the top pooled description out again whenever its Sim is gone, with no check that it is still a live NPC; a description with no household then throws `NullReferenceException` in `Motives.CreateMotives` and `SimDescription.Instantiate` for a taxi, limo, bus or fire-truck driver; the hook pops the bad entries off the top of the pool EA reads, and EA reuses the next good one or makes a new driver; counters 2041-2042, ledger §912, repro `npc-driver-pool-check` (queue `npc-driver-pool.json`, original core).
**Book carry 2026-11-01 (docs/515, repro PASS 2026-11-01 on Baseline-BP):** `bookcarry` (off by default): in the standing and sitting branch, `Book.StopUsingBook` clears `Sim.CarryStateMachine` without asking the carry machine `ReadBook.GetBook` made to exit, so the machine keeps its engine driver until the garbage collector finalizes it; the hook ends and disposes it. Not the cause of the stuck-arm pose a player saw (refuted live). Counters 2043-2044, ledger §913, repro `book-carry-check`, queue `book-carry.json`.

## HANDOFF 2026-10-26 ~11:15 PHT: memory / Error 12 work (read this first in a new session)

**Goals (Rodney 2026-10-26, memory `memory-goals-2026-10-26`):** find leaks and fix them; find unbounded growth and move it out of the game's address space; better cleanups from metrics; right-size after cleanups. **Only work that helps Error 12 (32-bit address space: free, largest free block, fragmentation) counts**; RAM/commit-only savings are out (GC free-block decommit stopped). Memory work uses **stress cycles** (full/empty marks + forced GC), not long natural soaks. Metrics first, fixes after. Apex-area experiments stay off and are proposed to Apex only with a proven path.

**Tools built today (all test-run, off by default unless noted):** `[MemDiag] Light` player memory lines (0.36.79, on in release ini; `diag: mem`, `diag: memsus`, destroyed-but-alive, `tools/memory-report.ts`, `--steady <prefix>`); `[MemDiag] AllocSites` per-call-site native tracking (docs/201; 0.36.85 `game-copy/ts3native.asi.allocsites3` fixes the caller attribution, 0.36.84 the stack-scan fault); `[MemDiag] TypeGrowth` Cork-style (docs/199; 0.36.83 `asi.typegrowth2` fixes the silent per-pass fault and 2-8 s cost); `[ResourceCache] TrimAfterCleanup` (docs/198, 0.36.80); `[GC] SqrtHeapLimit` (never acts: live managed heap 80-93 MB; dropped). Generators: `tools/cc-gen.ts` (objects, CAS, `--kind build` patterns/doors/windows/fences/stairs/terrain, `inv`, `makeup`, `presets`), `tools/cc-gen-script.ts` (per-item IL-cloned script mods, set `cc-script-100b`), `tools/cc-lot-gen.ts` (file-rewritten CC library lots). Sets in gitignored `game-copy/cc-synth/`. Stress actions: live place/wear/inventory cycles, `bb_cc_cycle`, `build_cc_cycle` (patterns, 3-floor building, 72 doors + stairs), `et_lot_cycle` (library lots), makeup/preset cycles, steady-state same/fresh 6 cycles.

**Results so far:** largest free drops in 160-220 MB one-time jumps, not a steady per-item leak; removal never gives largest free back (0-25 MB free back); presets flat; Mono heap flat; growth sits in allocator reservations (EA heap cores 492 MB / 33 reserves, Mono heap 380 MB, mimalloc commit +17 MB). Small Mono heap start (`InitialHeapMB=160`) gave ~+200 MB largest free (one load each, docs/199 §7) — parked fix candidate. Static (docs/201 §12): EA cores reserved bottom-up with no hint; top-down for short-lived big blocks is the real fragmentation lever but conflicts with Apex's hook at `0x4E5306` (proposal only). Scripted CC crash at startup was the generator (`<PrivateImplementationDetails>` data vs simler90's rebuilt DLLs, fixed in `cc-script-100b`, docs/67 §19). cc-lots "crash" Q was a hang guard kill during EA's long lot import (docs/67 §20).

**AllocSites results 2026-10-26 (docs/201 §13, ledger §363):** attribution works (hooks 4/4 + 5, drops/busy/nofree 0, no own frames). No per-object leak, no hole fragmentation: largest free is lost to permanent reservations growing to new peaks, cut bottom-up from the one big gap. CC (big 1,597 -> 1,237): EA heap cores +96 MB (triggered by EAStreamMemory, PackedStream, ClipResource; Apex area), NVIDIA/DXVK `nvoglv32` 128 MB reserve +128 once (largest step; the `miRes` "mimalloc" jump is this driver), Boehm +35, `Scripting/Simulator` Mono sub-heap cores +24 (ours), untracked +64 at load. Buy cycles lose 0; Build cycles -32/-16/-16 to untracked 16 MB blocks. Map views = bounded DXVK cache (no trend). Travel (1 trip of 3, job FAIL): -21 MB, at the save after home; Mono sub-heap +13 MB, Mono malloc +10.5. Heap leak candidate `CAS/GeometryResource` ~5 MB/cycle [inferred]. **P6 ladder rerun read 2026-10-25 (docs/189 §17, existing run data, no build, no run): P6 gain line has no measured path; recommend stopping it; Rodney stopped it 2026-10-25 ~21:40 PHT, see the decision above.** IP in-load ABBA, 6 valid rungs + hold2 crash: sim job wait 143-152 ms/s off on every rung, no on arm below that spread (StaleLotFp +9.8, all kinds +27). Why, from per-slice counters: a taken job costs the sim ~1.9 ms vs ~8 ms for a live one, but only 20-27% of armed jobs are taken and the 41-60% stale-refused cost ~10 ms each (more than the live plan they replace), so take x 6 ms minus stale x 2 ms nets ~0. StaleLot: 56-68% of armed jobs refused before any clone run by a world-level write (`stlNoRun`), after the run per-lot-only = 0 (footprint kind), with StaleLotFp the floor moves to lot-0 open-ground churn (`stlWorld_10`); window age 11-18 ms. AskLent/DoorMemoX: the "host" class is whole pump waits with one served call; service is 2.5 ms/s (~17 us per call); -43% asks (memo 47% hits, 0 mismatches) moved nothing. HoldKeyed: taken jobs' gate waits are 1-2.4 ms/s; the big gate waiter is site 12 (29-44 ms/s, 67 with all kinds), gate 10 is 0.1-3.4. Refuted: §2 rank 1, §1 "gate 10 biggest", §3.3 10-20% stale, §3.6 74 -> 15-25. Next (cheap): (1) keep every P6 switch off, drop P6 timing rungs, fix Crashes M/N/O first if FreeLock stays; (2) ~1 h counter: kind of the early-refusal write + lot-0 Sim vs object split, rides along a validation job; (3) routing time to gate 12 / F-G: smoke + correctness of `[PlacementRO]` on `ip-placement-crowd-loop`, judged by IP natural >= 300 ms count.

**Native leak scan (2026-10-25, docs/193, static, docs/191 step 2; pass 2 closed the open items):** catalogue `CustomContentIcon` pin **refuted**: the native grid clear sends detach `0x11` to every descendant and custom controls default `DisposeOnDetach` on (ctors `0x77E9E0`/`0x77EB20`/`0x77ED20`), so icons are disposed on clear [verified static, message-to-event dispatch inferred]. **P6 and the `+0x918` hold (docs/94 §24.11, ledger §336, static + run data):** a taken job holds the lock only for parts: the IC body outside the planner, door-ask retakes, the final retake, and acquires that are not own-only. That is ~1.9 ms BP / ~2.4 ms IP, against ~5.6 / ~9.7 ms with the game's plan. It stays `A_RUNNING` for the whole job, so gate 10, gate-0 misses, gate-12 writers and job waits still wait (on BP the span is longer: copy + clone). Gross gain ~2.4 / ~6.1 ms of sim wait per taken job, ~6-8 / ~16-21 ms/s at the measured take rate. P5c stale refusals cap it at 67% / 50%. Not visible in verify2 (noise). Next: the repro with VerifyEvery 0, `[MovedWaits]` on and `--ab routesnapplan`. Design candidates: the own-only copy before the hold and `A_RUNNING`, and stale rebase instead of refusal.

**PLAN RETARGETED TO THE NEW TEST METHOD (Rodney 2026-10-23; read this first).** How we work now:
1. **Discover** real player hitches only from natural play (real-hitch classifier: docs/161 §6; visible freezes + frames: docs/162/167) and the story fuzzer at night (docs/171). A natural run is discovery or a final per-release check, never a fix's test.
2. **Name the cause** per hitch (stage, script task, wait key, lock holder, sync-plan phase, frame service), then **force it**: a repro loop in the real save that fires the cause many times per load (shortcut style, docs/169), setup steps marked.
3. **Fix and test in minutes:** verify job (short, shadow compares) + loop A/B in one load, ABBA, >= 10 firings per arm, fixed event-count stop, confidence intervals (docs/168).
4. **Moved-waits loop:** read the A/B's moved-waits table; every cause that GREW in the on arm is the next target, same repro, fixes combined into one arm until the arm is clean (memory moved-waits-loop).
5. **Release:** `tools/release-check.ts` (smoke, save matrix, one forced loop per shipped fix, bundle A/B on repros, soak), ~2.5-3 h; no long night pairs.
Current targets in that order: (a) the lock-taker bundle for SV/IP 1-2 s freezes: SteerRO+AvoidRO+Gate0RO+DeferFootprint(2)+RouteToObjRO (steer-pass-ab.json, docs/175) + PlacementRO (docs/174, build after the chain) + ForeignKinds (docs/176): verify, then one bundle loop A/B, then the moved-waits loop; (b) SV 7 s crowd freeze: sv-crowd-cause run, then its repro; (c) overwatchchain (BP's only remaining >= 300 ms natural hitch); (d) crash 0x567121 reruns, then an Apex issue/PR if it is the game's own bug; (e) go/no-go study of a structural fix for the +0x918 lock (read-only routing snapshot, design E docs/82) since single read-only fixes keep moving the wait; (f) story fuzzer merge + smoke + believability lint; (g) Tuesday release via release-check. Superseded: fixed 10-min night pairs, natural-play A/Bs, ms/s-only targets.

**Several repros per load + session reuse (Rodney 2026-10-22: cut test time; docs/170 §1-2, ledger §272; self-check + dry-run, NOT run):** a profile job's `repros: [...]` runs those repros back to back in one load, one window each with its own tag `<job tag>-<id>`, its own pass rules and its own ts3native.log slice. `reuseSession` (default off) lets consecutive profile jobs with the same game start share the running game. Between windows every ts3fixes and ts3native switch goes back to its after-load value, and a world snapshot (clock, Sims, household, lot objects, VAS) is checked against limits. The next window gets a fresh start past a limit, after `maxJobs`, or with a stuck Sim. A failure restarts fresh at night and stops the queue in the daytime. The first minute is excluded only after a load (`<tag>-session.json`); otherwise a 15 s start edge. There is one frames session per load. Estimate: ~2-3 min plus 45 s of real data per reused window. **Holder site 0 fixed 2026-10-22 (docs/165 §8, ledger §265; `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0331` = asi.0330 + this, ts3native 0.36.26, counters v98, sha256 `380f90054a65f4a5de49dce0843aa4e24f8e170205f4c962dedfaca41498f152`, NOT run):** the `asi.0330` Holders run logged 2 of 4 waits with holder site 0 (AsyncLoader `[this+0x40]` and ResourceMan `GetPackageSource` `[this+0x48]` locks, both passed between threads). Causes: the holder wrote its entry after its own record and log line, the waiter read the table only at wait start, and the entry was cleared on release before a re-acquire; direct-mapped collisions were possible too. Fix: the entry is written first, the waiter reads it again when its acquire returns, entries are never cleared (the release hook has no Holders test now), and the table is 4-way set-associative. Every acquire is `Lock_Acquire 0x4E16F0` (no inlined copies; it returns the new count). Selftest has one holder mode per cause; selftest, steerro x4, hook-sites and check-docs-code pass. Next: one `Holders=1` run on asi.0331, expecting `hoNoSite` near 0.
**F1 lock-hook fast path + lock holders built 2026-10-22 (docs/165 §7, ledger §261; `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0330` = asi.0329 + this, ts3native 0.36.25, counters v98, sha256 `49e006433331ebcdd913f8486e04a46d350dd50a5456500e9fe698bf013479ca`, NOT run):**
- F1: `LockAcquireHook`/`LockReleaseHook` send a lock other than `+0x918`, taken by a thread that is not the job owner or the sync-session thread and holds no QL/SlotEnabledRO hold, straight to the original. There is no key, `GetCurrentThreadId`, SEH or P5b work on that path.
- The owner and `+0x918` paths are unchanged.
- The watchdog's `__try` moved into `LockOwnerTid`.
- Selftest bench (hot cache, noisy machine): hook overhead per non-918 pair went from +2.6..+8.2 ns to +1.0..+1.6 ns. Contended runs show no difference beyond noise.
- `[HitchAttr] Holders=1` (default 0, `HoldersMinMs=20`) records sim and main waits >= 20 ms on any game lock other than `+0x918`, with the holder's thread role and acquire site. Records go to the `ts3native_lockholders` ring, the hitch line's `holders` field (stutter-profile.js) and `worst-hitches.ts cause().holder`. It costs ~3-4 ns per pair while on, and a two-thread selftest covers it.
- Checks passed: full selftest, steerro-test default + PHASES + SYNCPHASES, `hook-sites --check`, `check-docs-code`.
- Next: §5.3 rerun on a clean night (main-thread ts3native share should be <= 1%), plus one `Holders=1` run.

**Main-thread waits 2026-10-22 (docs/165, ledger §260; asi.0329 rebuilt with /MAP (byte-identical), `bp-freeze-pan` mainstack, no game run):** the main thread's ts3native frames are one pass-through chain (`LockAcquireHook+0x1d7` -> `LockAcquireAsync+0x3cc` -> `P5bLock+0x52` -> `g_lockOrig`; `+0x596f0` = SEH handler, not a frame); it never reaches a gate, pump, H2/TailUnlock/P5b or `+0x918` wait. The "callers" `TS3W+0xbfd74c` etc. are `.rdata` EA allocator names (ResourceMan, AsyncLoader, Refpack, Cache): resource-system locks, game's own. Real blocking 1.5% (~0.5 ms/frame; 2.7% with the allocator CS); **our cost = hook overhead 4.9% of main-thread samples (~1.75 ms of a 36 ms frame)**. Next: F1 fast path at the top of `LockAcquireHook`/`LockReleaseHook` for non-owner threads and locks other than `+0x918`, SEH watchdog moved out of the common path (~30-45 min + selftest, est. -1.2..-1.5 ms/frame); size it with a selftest micro-benchmark, optional `[MainLockProbe]` counters (§5.2). Gate exemption for the main thread = 0 gain, do not build. Game lock/allocator traffic (~12 ms/frame) goes to Apex.
**Real hitches only 2026-10-22 (docs/161 §2 + §6, ledger §258; existing captures, no game run):** one shared classifier (`worst-hitches.ts exclusion()/runContext()/runInvalid()`) used by `hitch-ab.ts`, `freeze-report.ts` and `worst-hitches.ts`: every slow iteration / publish gap is real or excluded (first minute, profiler edge, repro-triggered incl. test_env resets, sampler window, agent call, render-paced gap = no iteration >= 50 ms inside unless `ForegroundRender=1` and >= 2 publish-less boundaries); counts, p99/p99.9, max and minutes from real ones, an excluded table beside them; noisy (others >= 10% of game CPUs, other CPU or clock drop), cut-short and crashed runs named and left out of pairs/pools. profile-world now logs `<tag>-calls.jsonl` (every agent call span) and `<tag>-samplers.jsonl`. Real-only baseline per 10 min >= 300 / >= 1000 ms: BP play 5.4 / 0.4, BP crowd 2.8 / 0, IP play 20.1 / 0.5, SV play 1.9 / 0, SV crowd 9.0 / 2.6 (3 valid runs, max 7.4 s); 15 of 43 runs invalid (14 noisy); real minutes ~30% of profiled. **For hitch baselines run without samplers (`--no-samplers`) on a quiet machine.**

**Script hitches named without samplers 2026-10-22 (docs/166, ledger §259; dummy PASS, NOT run in game):** `stutter-profile.js` times every task run inside the four seams with a CModule (`0xD7F000` task resume, `0xEA1090` runtime invoke for ScriptTick/OneShot, `0xE53620` yield frame save; sim thread, outermost run only, no thread suspended) and writes the 5 slowest runs >= 2 ms into each hitch line as `tasks: [{stage, name, ms, from, to}]` (name = class, ScriptProxy target or wrapped delegate method; from/to = resume and yield frames) plus `taskRuns`. **P5 lending gain model 2026-10-22 (docs/158, ledger §240; static + existing run data, `tools/p5-gain-model.ts`, NOT run):** P5 lends only during async ReplanFromPosition jobs, so in the release config (A10 off) every lending arm cuts 0. With A10 RFP on, the window is 17-23% of an eligible job (BP; IP 35%), ~5 ms per job; admitted takers carry 55-60% (P5b) / 86-89% (P5c) of the BP keyed wait, but by conservation the sim uses at most q <= 0.04-0.17 (P5b) / 0.22-0.5 (P5c) of the lent time, so the lending term is <= 0.4 / 3.1 ms/s BP. P5 itself lengthens each eligible job (+3.8..+9.6 ms measured with P5c on 0.35.25; P5b alone +5.6..+13 inferred), which outweighs it: P5b alone +5..+10 ms/s wait (worse, inferred), P5c -6..+7 (measured +3.0 mean), B2 vs B1 +1..+4. **Next (no build):** `snap-p5c-ab.json` on the current asi as day jobs per world with FreeLock=0 and FreeLock=1, read with `node tools/p5-gain-model.ts <tag>`; if dT >= ~3.5 ms per eligible job on both worlds, spend no clean night on P5b/P5c lending (work goes to the take path or the docs/150 hold cuts); only if dT <= 0 build the per-job wait counters of docs/158 §5 (q).
**P5 clone writer audit 2026-10-22 (docs/156, ledger §241; static, nothing built, NOT run):** every writer of every clone search input checked against the replay. One verified unrecorded writer that changes the answer: `Footprint_SetLockKeyObject 0x807110` (lock-key ICs via `0x82B2B0`) sets `fp+0x20` on an inserted footprint with no W1/W2 and no signature change, so a door/portal footprint that gets its first lock-key set keeps "no keys" in the clone (in-room class `0x807090`) until re-insert, full rebase or epoch (I-156-1). Polygon caches: the only valid-byte clear in the image is `0x80E0B0` (W6e), with no caller found, so I-94-381's refill path has no static caller (I-156-2/3); W6d/W6f never change an inserted footprint in place (I-156-4). Worker W6b on live footprints in clone runs is a live-state race (I-156-5). Next: F1 (7-byte stub on `0x807110`, force the footprint's lots full) + F2 (`snW6e` counter and force in the existing W6e row), ~1.5-2 h, one replay.cpp round; then F3 (EnsureAll at the armed cut on live grid/request footprints, ~1 h).

**Unseen throttle zero gain explained 2026-10-22 (docs/143 §8.15, measured, pair means of both slice orders):** the sim thread's `+0x918` wait is conserved per part (bounded by the route job's hold, ~115 ms/s), the CPU left to save is a few ms/s, and the avoidance skip costs ~10% it/s. Release default (`List=1`, `Avoid=0`, `Footprint=0`, `Rate=0`) keeps a small gain (~+5% it/s BP, route -8.8 ms/s). Real lever for the waits: shorter or lent hold (P5b/P5c, H2, S2); decision for Rodney. Single-order A/B jobs are not usable (slow middle slices). simBusy profiler (2c7294f) crashed frida-agent.dll: reverted in the working copy, needs a dummy-process test before reuse.

**FallbackAsync worst-hitch regression explained 2026-10-21 (docs/134 §10, ledger §214; static + logs):** the SV 1,027 / 541 ms frames of clean-night-0.2.0 are the sim thread waiting out one uncapped worker job (destroy `0x02000003`, steering `0x85FE10`); jobs > 400 ms only with `FallbackAsync=1` (up to 4,638 ms in freeze-fix-ab route-bits). Fix `[RouteAsync] FbJobCapMs` (ts3native 0.36.16, default 0, `--ab fbjobcap`, release-features `fbjobcap` = 300) + `long job` log line; A/B `tools/harness/queues/fbjobcap-ab.json` not run (asi `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0316b`). Recommendation: do not ship FallbackAsync+M1Retry on 0.36.14 as is; ship with FbJobCapMs after its A/B, else FallbackAsync=0 + SyncCapMs=1000.

**Mint fast paths 2026-10-21 (docs/139, static + monohost, no game run):** EA fuses superops only when a whole straight-line run equals one of its 22 sequences (`0xD89930` hashes the whole window), so `ldthis+ldfld.*` never fuse inside longer runs. Built in monohost: sub-run fusion (5-byte call patch at `0xE5C8CA`, `MONOHOST_MINTFUSE=sub`): dispatches -0.7% (EA `.cctor` code) to -11.8% (bench), pinned bench -7.1%, same checksum; 37/37 in 59 of 60 host runs (54 in a row), one unexplained `36/37` run with the check id lost. Threaded dispatch -1.7% (G-I10 closed, do not build); range-check removal no gain; `callvirt` cache misses 0.15-0.17% offline (two-entry cache and prefetch rejected, G-I5). Ranked: (1) sub-run fusion ~2-8 ms/s [inferred], 1-2 h port as `[MintFuse] Sub=1` default off + 2-load A/B night; (2) call/return fast path via jump-table entries ~15-30 ms/s [inferred], 8-14 h, in-load A/B; (3) new superops after G-I1, ~1-4 ms/s. Ledger docs/51 §194.

**Crash report tool built 2026-10-21 (offline, ts3native 0.36.7, docs/135, ledger §190; no game run):** `[CrashReport]` (shipped `Enabled=0`) writes a scrubbed `ts3native-crashesash-<UTC>-<pid>.txt` from an unhandled-exception filter that stays on top via the exe IAT slot of `SetUnhandledExceptionFilter` (`0xF9517C`, EA sets `0x4D7140` later [verified static]) and chains to it; `running.txt` marker gives a `-hard.txt` report next start; signature = fault + top 3 stack-scan frames, id `crashsig-<fnv32>`, title `[crash] TS3W+0x... (ts3native X)`. `tools/report-crash.ts` (`--self-check` in CI) shows exactly what would be sent, then own `gh` (search id, comment or create) or a prefilled `crash.yml` page. `.github/workflows/crash-issues.yml` labels `crash` + links same-id issues. P3 19 sites + ArenaFix + F1 installed (`p3Ready` 23, `n2mReady` 1, `finRuns` 73,809, `arenaRelNonEmpty` 0 in 5 min). **New blocker: P4 FrameTls SKIPPED in game: TlsAlloc returned indexes 102/103, not below 64** (the game process uses >64 TLS slots; the `fs:` rewrite only reaches the 64 inline slots). So no per-thread frame globals in game: the worker must not interpret (AutShadow Mode R jobs dropped from the queue). Fix: use TEB TlsExpansionSlots (`fs:[0xF94]` + (idx-64)*4) in the rebuilt blocks, or allocate the two keys before the game fills the inline slots (DllMain, early). I-120-1 confirmed again (sim thread registered, main not).

Not a missed interpreter global (core census = P4's two only). Fix `[MonoThread] ArenaFix=1` (with `GcLock=1`): clear after release-all, dedupe at install, drop in-use chunks before grow. **P3-B child-footprint soft misses, ts3native 0.35.8 (lane G) on master 0.35.7, counters v72, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.23, docs/51 §132, ~2 h):** 0.35.6 BP/IP rebased 865/654 lots (13.8/11.1 ms/s), 693/473 of them soft resync forces, every miss a child footprint with "clone copy: none". No game writer: `0x628220`/`0x665C50` have no pointer anywhere, so every child insert passes the hooked ApplyInsert (I-94-401 refuted). **P4 lock-key table release, ts3native 0.35.7 (lane F) on master 0.35.6, counters v71, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.22, docs/51 §131, ~1 h):** IP 0.35.5 refused every shadow `mat`/`copy` from record 1104 on: `PcAcquire` kept every lock-key table for the clone's life and refused at 64; since 0.35.5's soft resync the clone never rebuilds, so the cap never reset (reproduced in selftest at run 63). A full key apply now releases the older tables (only the newest stays). Next: `snap-p4` IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.laneF`; pass: no `mat`/`copy` run, 0 M1-M3.

**P4 `nover`/`busy` cut sharing, ts3native 0.35.6 (lane D) on master 0.35.5, counters v71, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.21, docs/51 §130, ~2 h):** 0.35.2's `nover`/`busy` (BP 708, IP 140) is all STARTs while a job runs (`p4QCutMiss` 0). Such a START now shares the newest pending cut (`PcBorrow`): that cut's version and write count, so any write after it makes the record stale (M5); the slot's hold counts both shadows; a first shadow that wrote clone lots refuses the second (`mat`/`stale`). Also `w0` is now read inside the cut's `+0x918` hold (a queued job could write before the old read). New `p4Borrow`, `p4BorrowMiss`, `p4BorrowDirty`. Next: `snap-p4` BP/IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.laneD`; pass: `nover`/`busy` far down, `p4Borrow` > 0, 0 M1-M3.

**`TestP5cIn` flake, ts3native 0.35.7 on master 0.35.5, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.12, docs/51 §134, ~2.5 h):** the bad-at-7 save failure is a test race: the fake worker started jobs without ServeAsync's `g_rgOffSimOpen` Dekker check, and the test's save and destroy skipped `RtEnter`'s hold. **Selftest 50-clean gate, ts3native 0.35.11 on master 0.35.10, tests only, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.14, docs/51 §135, ~1.5 h):** every failure was in the tests. P4 M1 (about 8-10%): the queued-STARTs phase wrote `RfStep`'s unrecorded kinds while the other job sat between its cut and live plan (with that change off: M1 in 4 of 40, all in that phase); now recorded writes only then. `TestP5cIn` hand-over: seen once under load as `hand at -1`, hand-overs +0 (the tripwire never fired in jobs 380-389, no P5c fault); jobs now go on until it fires. P5b steering ratio (most loaded runs) now counts only waits started while the window is open; rare P5b classes go first in each phase; queued-STARTs coverage keeps every 4th job unwoken; per-process temp files for parallel lanes. 150 clean runs in a row (4 and 6 lanes beside the live game). **Replay memory and consumer holds, ts3native 0.35.12 on master 0.35.11, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.15, docs/51 §136, ~3 h):** `snap-p4-long` on 0.35.9 was correct (0 M1-M3, 0 mismatches) but the replay clone hit `ReplayCapMB` at ~13 min (131 / 134 MB), then every shadow was M4 `nover/off`. Fixed: the replay's pointer tables doubled forever under key churn (erased slots counted as used; verified offline, fake `fps` 1024 -> 4096 slots); one `RP_LOTS` batch in flight (each carries the whole map order, 2.5 MB on BP; queued batches behind the lagging consumer were the bulk of the growth, inferred); `plOverlap` = the sim's cut using the private pools during a `Mode=1` shadow, now one owner (`g_plSet`: the cut only tries, busy = deferred, P4 refused `noclone`); consumer `RpUnify` / map resync no longer rebuild tables of every copy under `+0x918`, GC every 16th hold; the cut cost no longer counts the replay audit hashing (BP 10.1 of 23.4 ms/s); rebases walk the map once less. `mat/timeout` should follow the lag (inferred). Selftest `TestReplayLongRun` + `TestPools` ok, steerro-test ok. Next: rerun `snap-p4-long` BP + IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snap03512` on a quiet machine; pass: no cap switch-off in 20+ min, `plOverlap` 0, holds and `mat/timeout` down, 0 M1-M3. Still open: the whole-map order per batch and its resync (delta format next), P3 image cost in the cut.

**`snap-p4-long` 0.35.12 follow-up, ts3native 0.35.13 on master 0.35.12, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.16, docs/51 §137, ~3.5 h):** 0.35.12 fixed memory (BP 62.7 / IP 76.8 MB). (1) IP lot mismatch: the same lot `0x08F300536B484DF0` Q 1, clone 9 entries more, on 0.35.9 and 0.35.12; it stayed wrong until a whole rebuild because an audit mismatch forced nothing. Now a mismatched lot is rebased at the next cut, and the next hold logs the entries only one side has ("audit diff"), so the next run names the writer (still unknown). (2) BP M2 plan 43: the same door pair and costs as 0.33.1/0.33.3, now with `[OptsInit] Enabled=1`; the ctor and the hub compare `0x64FCE0` were checked statically, so the stack-hub cause (I-94-380) is refuted for this plan. Lot `0x6C110033` permission or portal data is the next suspect; not fixed. (3) `noclone`: the cut took the private pool set even with nothing to rebase; now only a rebase or build takes it (the batch-in-flight wait is unchanged). (4) Holds: EPOCH (115-130 ms) and full-batch (182-206 ms) frees of replaced clone memory now go to a grave freed in ~1 ms slices; a 5-minute log splits the consumer's lock time by record type. (5) Cut / sim cost: not changed (all-lot rebases after a P4 release with an unknown dirty set, P3 image builds). Selftest ok (forced rebase + diff, grave), steerro-test ok. Next: rerun `snap-p4-long` BP + IP and `snap-p3b` IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snap03513`; pass: no repeated lot mismatch, "audit diff" lines, `noclone` down, no "record type 5 applied in" lines, 0 M1-M3 apart from plan 43.

**IP lot mismatch: live bounds in REM records, ts3native 0.35.18 (set at merge) on master 0.35.17, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.21, docs/51 §147, ~1.8 h):** the `0x08F300536B484DF0` Q 1 mismatch starts after a whole clone rebuild that follows a P4 "ApplyLotUpdate in a clone run" discard (IP 1856, 0210). `0x666010` skips leaves when the `+0x2C` answer misses the Q rect; the three classes answer stored box floats (`0x620E90`), the floor level cache (`0x80EF80`) and the parent's answer (child `0x806C50`) [verified]. The W2 pre-hook now carries live `+0x2C` answers in the REM record; the replay compares each with the copy's, logs both rects and forces the lot on a difference (I-94-479). **P5 / P5b / P5c ready for live, ts3native 0.35.19 on master 0.35.18, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.22, docs/51 §149, ~2.3 h):** audited against 0.35.12-0.35.18 (one batch in flight, pool owner `g_plSet`, clone grave, two-bucket unify, clone-run lot attribution, flat bounds, shell refresh, REM live bounds): one broken assumption. Mode=2 acquires the clone inside the job's `+0x918`, but the replay consumer applies the cut's batch only under `+0x918`, so a consumer not yet at the cut when the job took the lock made the acquire spin 50 ms with the lock held, refuse `mat/timeout` and plan with the game (and a consumer blocked in `Lock_Acquire` refuses the P5b window). Fixed: `P5PreLock` in ServeAsync waits, holding nothing, until the consumer is at the job's cut and idle (bounded 50 ms). Everything else verified unchanged (table in §22.22.1). Selftest `TestP5PreLock`; selftest 100 / 100 clean (50 on 0.35.17, 50 rebased on 0.35.18, 4 lanes). Queues (all `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snapp5`, counters 72, HitchAttr on): `snap-p5-shadow` (Mode=2, VerifyEvery=1: nothing taken) -> `snap-p5` -> `snap-p5b` (ReleaseLock + ReleaseGates) -> `snap-p5c` (FreeLock, hitch-attr env) -> `snap-p5c-ab` (`--ab routesnap`, clean night). **Next:** park the 0.35.19 asi as `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snapp5`, run the chain in that order, one BP + IP pair per step, stop on any crash / `Mode=2 off` / hand-over line; read `snap-audit-report.ts` (P5, P5b, P5c blocks and gates), `shadow-compare-report.ts` (P5 verify line), `hitch-attr-report.ts` (ms/s vs BP 68 / IP 73); checklist docs/94 §22.22.4.

**Replay cut split and signature mix, ts3native 0.35.20 on master 0.35.19, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.23, docs/51 §151, ~1.7 h):** 0.35.17 BP 0303 (clean): cut 9.3 ms/s, "lots (replay)" 6.8; of all cut time 35% are 15 whole clone builds (150-219 ms) asked by P4 discards (`PcRelease` `P4REL_DISCARD` after lazy stops, = `snPcDiscard` 15), 6% rebases, and ~0.6-0.7 ms per cut the per-cut scan. **Routing round on the 0.35.22 BP runs: live-footprint W6b, shells past deferrals, bnm share, `ReplaySigMs`, `LotsUnlocked`, ts3native 0.35.23 on master 0.35.22, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.26, docs/51 §160, ~2.9 h):** BP 0444 / 0458 (`LazyKeep=1`): no fault after 10+ keep releases each (I-94-492: two clean runs); M2 1 / 0, M4 5.32 / 4.76%, cut + sim 5.27-5.96 / 4.32-6.37 cyc-ms/s. Both runs' worst hold (181 / 157 ms) and most of the window's rebase cost and `noclone` burst came from one W6b "write with no lot": a live footprint (not a clone copy), now no rebase at all (logged). Shells-only rebases go out past a map batch in flight or a busy pool set (fewer deferred cuts, `noclone`). bnm: an equal hash-order walk shares the old image without the sort (bench 173 -> 40 us). New switches, default 0: `[RouteSnap] ReplaySigMs` (signature scan at most every N ms, unscanned lots keep their old signatures; 250 = trial, ~-0.8 ms/s) and `LotsUnlocked` (RP_LOTS applied outside `+0x918`, holding `g_rpUse` + the pool set: max hold ~11 ms instead of 45-55). The cut-split line's tail (cyc, quiet, sig) moved to "snap replay cut split (2)" (Log cut it at ~510 chars). Expected BP cut + sim ~3.7-4.4 cyc-ms/s on defaults, ~3.0-3.6 with `ReplaySigMs=250`; M4 ~2.5-4%. selftest ok (`TestBnmShare`, `TestReplay03523`, `TestPcLazyKeep` live W6b), steerro-test ok. **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP (+ IP) on the 0.35.23 asi with `LazyKeep=1` (W6b live lines, no 82-lot batch, cost lines), then one BP each with `ReplaySigMs=250` and `LotsUnlocked=1`.

**BP door M2 diagnostic: `[RouteSnap] DoorDump` (default 0), log-only, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.27, docs/51 §162, ~1.5 h):** the door answer asks script only when the portal owner's lot graph has `+0x8F` set (`0x64F46A`); the clone's lot copies keep the `+0x8F` of their last rebase because `RpSig` and the P3 image leave it out (I-94-500, verified). Suspect for the recurring M2 at goal 1261.20 / 1758.27 (I-94-464): lot `0x6C110033...` clone 0 / live 1, so the clone never asks and walks door `...7D22F4D0` (I-94-499, inferred). The dump (p4door.h) on an M2/M3 or a goal in `DoorBox`: the unshared door ids, the live asks with `used`, every lot whose `+0x8F` differs (clone vs live at the plan vs live now), both sides' portal records, the door mirror. selftest ok (`TestP4DoorDump`). **Next:** BP `snap-p4-long` with `DoorDump=1 DoorBox=1260,1757,1262,1759`; if `f8fDiff` names that lot, add `g[0x8F]` to `RpSig` (one line, replay.cpp) and confirm in one BP.

**Lot `+0x8F` in `RpSig`, ts3native 0.35.24 on master 0.35.23, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.28, docs/51 I-94-499, ~0.5 h):** built ahead of the door-dump run: a lone `+0x8F` flip now changes the lot signature, so the next scan sends a shell refresh and the clone takes the live byte (`RpLotShell` copies the whole node). No event-driven mark (E11 has no lot key; `ReplaySigMs`=0 scans every cut). selftest ok (`TestReplaySigMix`: 6 lone flips, old signature blind, new one rebases once and the clone byte matches), steerro-test ok. **Next:** BP `snap-p4-long` on 0.35.24 with `DoorDump=1 DoorBox=1260,1757,1262,1759`: expect no M2 at 1261.20 / 1758.27, empty `f8fDiff`, `rpRebaseWhy[2]` near 0.35.23.

**0.35.21 crash fixed: freed A\* object on clone Qs after a lazy keep, ts3native 0.35.22 on master 0.35.21, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.25, docs/51 I-94-492, ~1.3 h):** BP 0419 (noisy) crashed 3.4 min in, after the first lazy-keep release: `0x664396` is the Q's A\* node-hash lookup (`Q+0x28`); the release dropped (freed) the A\* object but kept the clone, and the acquire only re-points Qs holding the live or current object, so the Qs kept the freed one and later runs wrote into freed EA heap (`0x4E5EC6` free-list unlink) [cause verified in disassembly + source; heap link inferred]. Also older pending cuts (parked / second slot) acquired before the rebase. Fixed: dropped object remembered and re-pointed at the next acquire (also the old WRAP path); a keep release refuses every pending cut; `[RouteSnap] LazyKeep` default 0 (off: every lazy stop rebuilds as before). Selftest `TestPcLazyKeep` (d) with negative control; `hook-sites.ts --check` ok (docs/68 row `0x80EF80`). **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP with `LazyKeep=1`, two clean runs before calling it fixed; the rest of the 0.35.21 checks below.

**Cut cost round: P4 lazy keep, lot-named W6b / W3, quiet-cut reuse, REM bounds patch, cycle costs, ts3native 0.35.21 on master 0.35.20, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.24, docs/51 §153, ~3.2 h):** BP 0303: 35% of cut time was 15 whole-clone builds after P4 lazy stops. Disassembly: before `ApplyLotUpdate` its callers write only that lot (`0x82B810`: built and room bytes, then W4), the quadtree-deferral callers only hooked leaves / new objects / the Q's lot; so those two stop kinds put their lot in the dirty list and release `P4REL_LAZY` (lots rebased, A* + W' dropped, unwound Q pins `+0x48` cleared and their lots forced); other stop kinds still rebuild. W6b in a clone run names its footprint's lots from the clone map (was force-all, ~180 ms batches); W3 names its lot. `[RouteSnap] ReplayQuietMs` (default 0 = off; trial 250): cuts with nothing recorded skip the lot walk and signature scan (SYNC kept), full scan at least every N ms; the split line counts late catches. IP 0341 REM differences (256): `0x80EF80` level-box inputs that change unrecorded (child `+0x10` z: 0.2-2.0 widening; 0.001 box drift) now travel in the REM record and are patched into the copy before the remove (no forced lot); a differing child-list state stays forced. New 5-min "snap cut cost" line (wall and thread-CPU cut + sim) and cyc-ms/s on the replay split and consumer lines (QueryThreadCycleTime / 2.3 GHz nominal) for noisy runs. Selftests `TestPcLazyKeep`, `TestReplayQuiet`, `TestRemPatch`, `TestCycles`; selftest ok, steerro-test ok. Expected BP cut ~3-5, cut + sim ~4-6 ms/s. **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP + IP on the 0.35.21 asi (correctness: 0 mismatches / M1-M3, "lazy stop released without a clone rebuild" vs "clone built ... p4clone.cpp" lines, no W6b no-lot lines, "patched to live" vs "not patchable"; cost from the cyc-ms/s lines), then one BP with `ReplayQuietMs=250` for the exposure line. Not done: bounded `RP_LOTS` slices (not cheap), other stop kinds.

**Shell refresh for lot graph builds, ts3native 0.35.17 on master 0.35.16, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.20, docs/51 §145, ~2.3 h):** `snap-p4-long` on 0.35.16 was correct (BP 10,589 / IP 5,875 + 5,202 compares, 0 M1-M3) and back at 0.35.13's rebase counts, but rebases still cost BP 5.6 / IP 10.2-10.4 ms/s (~11-12 ms per batch: whole-lot copy, two whole-map walks, the map order and its resync). The "forced by a write" lots (BP 389, IP 573 / 526) are `LotGraph_Build` (W4): STALE records = W4 count, ~89% from `PcPrepare`'s first use at a P4 START after a footprint change cleared `+0x8C`; every signature rebase had its Q list unchanged. W4's quadtree changes go through recorded W1 / W2 (new Qs through W3) [verified disassembly], so W4 and signature lots with an unchanged Q list now get a shell refresh (node, portals, rooms, hashes; the clone keeps its Qs, trees and map nodes); a shells-only batch walks no map and needs no resync. IP lot mismatch (0210, same lot, no "keeps leaves" line): the non-reaching remove case is left (I-94-478), not fixed; 0235 had none. `RP_LOTS` hold split not built (full batches should become rare); bnm not touched. **`bp-snap-p4` on 0.31.2 CRASHED (2026-10-19 08:21 PHT); fixed in ts3native 0.31.4, counters v57, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.11, docs/67 §6, ~3 h):** fault reading `0x7` at `0x63576A`: the game's lot-0 `LotRouting_RemoveObjectFootprints 0x635700` steps twice over an FME whose remove is pending in `P+0x3C` and walks past its range to the bucket sentinel [verified disassembly, report registers; reproduced on the game's code, `steerro-test` G2]; our stub there is clean; why the remove was pending is inferred. Fixes: flush the id's pending removes before a lot-0 remove (`lrPendFlush`, `lrPendBusy`); stop P4 clone runs before an insert/remove deferral on an iterated clone Q, which would write the live pending maps the clone P shares (`pcDeferStop`); `SnCloneProbe` no longer reads box-class floats as a cache pointer (11 first-chance AVs per load since 0.29.0 that filled the crash log); `P4 FAULT context` line (the 0.31.2 fault at `0x7CEF3688`, eip in heap, had none); clone builds log what asked for them. The `0x651374` fix held (83 clone runs). Next: `snap-p4` BP and IP on 0.31.4 (`minCounters` 57); pass = no crash, `lrPendBusy` 0, `pcDeferStop` 0 or a logged stop.
**`bp-snap-p4` on 0.32.4 (2026-10-19 10:52 PHT): first real P4 compares (3, all M0, 21.0 vs 20.9 ms), then crash H; fixed in ts3native 0.32.7, counters v57, offline, not run (docs/67 §10, docs/94 §21.13.2, docs/51 §114, ~3 h):** plan 5's clone run built an unbuilt room (`RoomGraph_FirstUse 0x63D2F0`), whose `+0x24` list grow EA-freed our `RpAlloc` copy (fault `0x4E5200`); the same build EA-frees `+0x34`'s inner buffers, which the flat copy kept live. The fault unwound past EA's heap `LeaveCriticalSection`, so the worker kept the heap lock and the game hung (heartbeat frozen, no xcpt). Fix: room lists `+0x24` / `+0x34` deep-copied as EA blocks (`RpRoomListsCopy`), `P4EaHeapUnlock` after a clone-run fault. steerro-test K7 (fails on the 0.32.6 copy), selftest, seeds 1-2 x 3000 ok. 3. **Destroys (`0x2000003`, 11.8% of the sim wait):** why a destroy waits on the job; can it be queued to the worker like design D (docs/84 §2.4) or deferred on the sim thread. **Studied (static) 2026-10-18, docs/88:** the key is our own S3b closure #4 (`RT_DESTROY` waits for the running job). Destroy touches no routing state (7 hops); the real dependency is object lifetime only: the job looks objects up with `FindById` (no AddRef) and `lock inc`s them, and the destroy's last `Release` can free them under it. Queue-to-worker and whole-destroy deferral rejected (managed free, script sees the object gone at once). Recommended **design G, destroy graveyard**: park one reference (`0x9395A0`), destroy now without the wait, release at the next IC point with no job (D's drain points); keep the wait for D-X records, a full graveyard, closing and non-sim threads. Gain cap 8.8 ms/s BP (3.1-4.3 IP), realistic 3-6 ms/s, ~1.5 d; offline steerro-test section I, then `bp-dg-shadow` (probe P-G: caller/kind split), `bp-dg-ab`, `ip-dg-ab` (`--ab destroygrave`). **Built standalone 2026-10-21 (docs/88 §10, counters v76 after the 0.36.11 merge, not run):** `[Discourage] DestroyPark` on P5c's graveyard (the 0.30.0 `[DestroyGrave]` branch `14a25ef` stays unmerged); queue `destroy-park-ab.json` (`--ab destroypark`).

Worker-applied, read-only and no-wait options rejected. Recommended **design U**: detour `0x833560`, on the sim while a job runs and `+0x918` is not owned run the early out now and record `(id, enable, arg)` in D's FIFO; D's drain applies it with `force = 1` when no job runs, D-X applies it at destroy (so design G keeps the wait for those ids unchanged). 15. **Gate-12 family F-B, slot position (`0x76F0A0`), ~12.7 ms/s BP (69% of gate 12):** `bp-p1b` today put the F-B gate-12 waits' second frame at `0x76F15D` inside `0x76F0A0` (the slot position helper of `IC_Slots_GetPositionOfSlotImpl` / `IC_RouteManager_IsSlotUnobstructed`), NOT in `IC_Slots_IsSlotEnabledImpl 0x76F000` (which touches neither `0x76F0A0` nor the lot table; the queue job name is kept). **Studied + built (static/offline) 2026-10-18, docs/90 (design SH, ts3native 0.22.2, counters v42; parked `game-copy/ts3native.asi.slotro` sha256 `2bdee20779c4`):** the gates (`LotTable_Contains` = gate 12, `GetOrInsert` = gate 0) are the only serialised part of `0x76F0A0`; their reads (lot table W5, lot entry portal vector W4) are worker-exclusive under QL. So `[SlotEnabledRO] Enabled=0 Mode=1 Shadow=0` detours `0x76F0A0` and runs the game's own body under a QL-shared try-lock: `RgEnter` passes gate 12 and gate-0 hits without the `+0x918` wait; any other gate site, a gate-0 miss, an odd portal (class or count < 1), or any Lock_Acquire / host / managed call drops the hold and waits as today. Portal vtable `0xFED048` AddRef/Release become interlocked while installed (the sim now AddRefs beside the worker). Offline: selftest `TestSlotRo` (interlocked counts incl. a 600k-pair 3-thread race, gate pass/drop, hook dispatch, fallbacks, shadow, unwind releases QL), steerro-test section I (the game's own `0x628A90`/`0x62EAB0`/`0x64E8E0`/`0x64E8F0`: 12000 gate decisions over seeds 1-4, 0 mismatch), hook-sites (docs/68 row), check-docs-code all pass. Night jobs `bp-slotro-shadow`, `bp-slotro-ab`, `ip-slotro-ab` (`--ab slotenabledro`) in `clean-night-2.json`. Gain cap ~12.7 ms/s BP for F-B; realistic less (the wait can reappear at the next routing touch). **Unverified live:** that W4/W5 plus sim-thread writers are the whole write set of the body's gated reads during a job (inherits L1's open point); the live gain. Equal answers of the held body are structural (SlCall runs the game's own body; the hold only skips the lock). **Live `bp-slotro-shadow` 2026-10-18 13:54 PHT (0.22.2, Shadow=1, PASS, load ok):** 46,541 calls, 45,543 dry holds (98%) passed gate 12 and 179,495 gate-0 hits inside them, 0 drops, 0 fallbacks, 0 shadow mismatches, interlocked portal refcounts on with 0 retries: SH covers nearly every call; ready for `bp-slotro-ab` / `ip-slotro-ab` at night. **Live `bp-slotro-ab` 2026-10-18 14:49 PHT (0.22.2, ABBA, shadow on, PASS, load ok, daytime): REGRESSION.** On vs off: 43.5 vs 52.2 it/s (-17%), hitches >=50 ms 191 vs 164/min, >=100 24 vs 18/min, stall 7.8 vs 6.5 s/min; every on slice below the neighbouring off slices (38-48 vs 55-59 it/s, first off slice 37 excluded as the oddity). slCalls 76,271, slTaken only 1,906, 0 mismatches. Likely cause [inferred]: holding QL shared over the body blocks the worker's QL-exclusive writers (W4/W5), so jobs run longer and the sim waits on them elsewhere. SH stays OFF; next: count worker QL-exclusive wait time per side before any change. **Live `ip-slotro-ab` 2026-10-18 15:10 PHT (PASS, load ok, daytime):** no gain, slightly worse tail: 67.1 vs 67.0 it/s, hitches >=50 ms 127 vs 126/min, >=200 12.9 vs 10.0/min, stall 10.1 vs 9.4 s/min.
16. **`RoutingWorld_RouteToObjectCheck` `0x83156D` (where SlotEnabledRO's gate-12 wait went, 9.0 ms/s BP on):** **Designed 2026-10-21 (static, no game run): docs/140 `[RouteToObjRO]`.** `0x83156D` is the outer `+0x918` take at `0x831568` [verified]; only `IsSlotUnobstructed` / `IsRouteToObjectUnobstructed` wait there. Three call-site retargets, clone of the route part `0x830340` (obstacle point query, target footprint lookup, BlockerCheck2d segment gather) under QL shared, slot helper via SH; needs SteerRO + SlotEnabledRO. ## Resume here (2026-10-16 23:15 PHT hand-off; superseded by the section above)

**2026-10-17 session: focus on new features with big gains, starting with routing off the sim thread (Rodney).**
- G11 IP measured: movable 6.3 of 65.9 plan ms/s (BP 14.2 of 55.1), so both under the docs/18 gates. Built anyway under the offload rule (stable steps accepted at <= ~2% loss). Almost every window close is gate site `0x62EAB0` (lot table find-or-insert, ~1,860 outermost unlocked sim-thread entries/s); every other site together ~10/s.
- **H6 S3b + M1 built** (ts3native 0.11.0, counters v26, `[RouteGate] Enforce=1`, parked `game-copy/ts3native.asi.h6m1` sha `a942c27fc757`, selftest ok; details docs/60 §2.3 status). Not run in game. **Ran 2026-10-17 (BP, docs/60 §2.3 status):** stable after 0.11.1 (0 crashes, 0 watchdogs, 0 stuck Sims seeded), but the seeded off/on pair is a clear regression: iterations/s -25%, hitches +36%, p99 iteration 645 vs 303 ms; the sim thread waits for the jobs (pump 36 + gates 6.5 + destroys 5 ms/s) more than the 33 ms/s of route time it sheds. Stays off; IP not run. Resume only when the steering closers are solved (docs/81, docs/82).
- **H6 W (new, Rodney's idea): handle the window closers so routing can really leave the sim thread.** For each closer: (a) a sync read that needs its answer now cannot simply move to the routing thread (it would wait behind the job), so make it lock-free when it touches no job-written state (e.g. a lot-table hit) or answer it from a snapshot; (b) a write the sim does not wait on can be queued to the routing thread and applied there in order, so its wait goes with the routing; (c) anything that reaches managed code stays on the sim thread. Check each candidate's transitive deps (locks, waits, managed and remote calls, sim-only state) up to **4 hops max** (Rodney, 2026-10-17; raised from 2 for the steering line): hop 1 = the candidate's own deps, hop 2 = the deps of anything hop 1 forces to move too; per candidate a verdict (movable / movable with named extra moves / stays on sim); deeper chains are listed as open and decided after. Static study done (docs/80): a lot-table hit writes nothing shared, but the busiest `0x62EAB0` callers (Sim steering) already took `+0x918` in `RoutingWorld_ObstacleQuery 0x82EDB0` earlier in the same function, so a lock-free `0x62EAB0` alone moves little. Native G11 in the BP M1 run agrees: windows closed by a `+0x918` acquire 83%, gate sites 9%, quadtree writes 8%; movable 1.8% of plan time. **Next H6 W target: the per-tick, per-walking-Sim steering query `0x82EDB0`** (docs/18 try-lock / snapshot option): 2-hop dependency table, what a skipped or stale obstacle answer does to Sims (overlap risk), then a measured prototype. Lot-table options (docs/80 §4) only after that. **Steering study done (docs/81):** the query writes shared state under the lock too (quadtree pin counter `Q+0x48`, deferred insert/remove flush `0x666A40`, footprint refcounts), and the job makes the same writes, so no reader lock or lock-free query; a skipped answer reads as "nothing in the way" for one tick (no cache, no retry), a stale one is mostly right. Recommended design: caller-level try-lock at `0x85FD10`/`0x846B70`/`0x85B470` (skip or reuse last tick's object ids, max 2 ticks, then wait). Gain cap (Poisson model, inferred): ~5.8 ms/s BP / ~7 ms/s IP if steering is all of the 83%, ~2 ms/s if half. Below the 15/30 gates: probes first (`vfG11CloseBy` at the 5 steering call sites, per-site `+0x918` holds, `vfStSkipDry`, `vfQtCount`), build only if steering dominates. **4-hop study done (docs/82):** steering reaches managed code through the agent vtable (`0x1044590` `+0x60`/`+0x64` -> `World_FireEvent` 0x3A/0x39, after `+0x918` is released, so no deadlock, but a skipped check drops that tick's route callbacks); a 4th race (per-footprint polygon cache `[fp+0x38]`, rebuilt by both steering and the job); `node+0x30` is plan-time only; lock order clean to 4 hops. Changing only steering's writes (atomic counters, coarse quadtree lock, queue to worker) does not free it. Best design **E**: the worker takes our own lock exclusively only around its quadtree/footprint/lot-table writes (~14 worker-only detours), steering runs read-only (no pin counter, flush, refcounts or cache rebuild; invalid cache falls back to the docs/81 skip); 6-9 days; alone <= ~5.8 BP / 7 IP ms/s, with the docs/80 gate fix and quadtree readers 12-27 ms/s BP only if steering closes >= ~75% of `+0x918` windows. Unverified basis: a footprint stays alive while it is in the quadtree. **Next: probes first** (`vfG11CloseBy` by steering call site, then the 7 docs/82 §9 counters) in one observe-only build + BP/IP run; decide E vs stop after that. **vfG11CloseBy measured 2026-10-17** (0.11.2, seeded 12345, async off, `h6-m1.json` bp/ip-g11-closeby, both PASS): one call site, `RouteFollow_EditPath 0x85F5A0` -> `GoalFootprintGather 0x82F0B0` (return `0x85F6CB`), causes **94.7% (BP) / 96.9% (IP)** of the movable time lost; next are gate 10 (goal step `0x75C2D0`) 2.7% / 2.8% and quadtree reinsert 2.3% / 0.1%; the obstacle query `0x82EDB0` sites barely appear. Movable today 1.0 of 60.1 s (BP), 0.9 of 96.9 s (IP). **Next: a read-only `0x82F0B0` during a job for that caller (other sites keep waiting), shadow verify outside jobs, tripwires (pin counter, polygon-cache rebuilds by the sim during a job), fallback to waiting when unsure; plus a worker job queue so other Sims' plans stop waiting on the single slot.** **Job queue built and run 2026-10-17** (0.11.3, counters v28, `bp-h6-queue-seed-on` PASS, same seed as the M1 pair): 8,742 jobs async (single slot: 3,815), 8 refused, 1 dropped, 0 skipped, queue depth max 4, 0 watchdogs, `vfRtQtBare` 0, 0 stuck Sims. Vs seed-off: iterations/s 26.3 vs 28.4 (-7%; single slot -25%), hitches 5.1 vs 4.8/s, p99 iteration 307 vs 303 ms, sim-thread route 50 vs 110 ms/s; the sim still waits 62 ms/s pumping (the `0x82F0B0` path edit), 16 ms/s at gates, 8 ms/s on destroys. **Probe P0 (0.11.4, 2026-10-17):** sync G11: 95% of closes keyed `0x85FFC7` = the plan's own `Route_PlanCommon` tail (runs on the worker in async mode), so the docs/81-83 steering share was a measurement artefact; locomotion `0x85FEDC` is not a closer. Async (queue on, seeded, `bp-p0-async` PASS, 10,996 jobs, 3 dropped): the sim waited ~74 ms/s; by site: discourage footprints `IC_RouteManager_Add/RemoveDiscourageFootprint` -> `0x834370`/`0x834860` 29%, steering obstacle query `0x82EDB0` from locomotion `0x85FE10` + avoidance `0x846D21` 26%, `LotTable_Contains` gate 12 15%, destroys 12%, `UpdateSimFootprint 0x833560` 5%, goal step 4%. `bp-p0-sync` froze game time after its `save_game` step (save itself result 0); one occurrence, logged; rerun to check. **Next: static study (5 hops) of the top three, then build: queue discourage footprint writes to the worker, read-only steering query, lock-free LotTable_Contains hit.** **Design D built and crashed 2026-10-17:** 0.12.0/0.12.1 called `ObjectService_FindByIdAddRef 0x9395A0` with the wrong argument count (it is `(idLo, idHi, Object** out)`, `ret 0xC`): stack corruption, silent death 10 s in (no EA report; the new `[Crash] Log=1` exception logger exists since 0.12.1). Fixed in 0.12.2, which then crashed applying a deferred Add: AV reading `0xFFFFFFFF` at `0xAB88BD` under the lot lookup `0xC62D40` called from `0x834519` (inside `AddDiscourageFootprint`), from our drain. 0.12.3 keeps the flags as whole dwords (0.12.0 truncated them to bytes) and sets `[Discourage] Enabled=0` by default. **Cause found (static, 2026-10-17):** the fault is `movaps xmm0,[eax]` at `0xAB88BD` (`0xADD740` <- `0x834514`) on the first point of our copied array; `movaps` needs 16-byte alignment and the copy came from `malloc` (8-byte aligned on 32-bit), so a #GP reported as an AV at `0xFFFFFFFF` (matches the logged info). Not the flags, not the object's state. 0.12.4 copies with `_aligned_malloc(...,16)`; the selftest fake asserts alignment. **Design D confirmed 2026-10-17** (two clean runs `bp-d-async-3`/`-4`, crash rule): same build 0.12.4, seeded, unfocused, vs async off `bp-d-off-same`: iterations/s 36.7/35.9 vs 32.7 (+10-12%), hitches 3.1/3.2 vs 3.8/s, sim-thread route 50 vs 119 ms/s, 12,203 jobs 0 dropped, 0 watchdogs, `vfRtQtBare` 0, 0 stuck Sims, 1,676 discourage calls deferred and all applied. Regression: p99 of the per-second max iteration 285/286 vs 259 ms. The earlier '-7% with the queue' compared a focused run with unfocused ones (invalid). **p99 rise analysed (hitch logs):** hitches > 200 ms 14 off vs 15/12 on, same mean length, worst-5 lists overlap, so mostly which few seconds land in the top 1%; one real part: locomotion inside long hitches 21 vs 4 ms = the steering query `0x82EDB0` waiting for a running job (P0's 22% site, docs/84 §3 design S2); hitches > 100 ms -43%. Next (Rodney): (2) done, (1) fresh off/on baseline in the realistic test_env plus Isla Paradiso, (3) the EditPath freeze root cause. Rule learned: read the disassembly of every game function we call, not only the ones we hook.

**H6 async routing build order (docs/60 §2, 2026-10-16):** S2b observe-only counters on all 15 gate sites + `vfRt*`; S3a terrain-swap source fix alone (parks the shape on `PhysShape_Release 0x682760` from the swap, frees on main when no ray in flight; also fixes the main-vs-sim race, fix #1); S3b all closures on in sync mode; S4 milestone M1 (async only in live mode, no build/buy/save, no pending lot updates, clean lot index); S5 lift limits one by one. Proxies replaced by worker-held locks (proxy = up to one sim tick). Hook sites shared with `[HeightCache]`/`[Verify]` need one shared stub per address.

**Rule (Rodney, 2026-10-16): mapping is not trusted until checked live.** After every mapping run: (1) add each runtime-checkable claim to the inference ledger (docs/51) with how to test it; (2) add the probe, counter or repro that can check it (`[Verify]` counters with the `vf` naming, agent actions + repros.json), or note why it is static only; (3) run the short `verify` queue and fill the verdicts (`verify-verdicts.ts --write`); (4) fixes build on claims that are confirmed by EITHER (a) a live check (verify counters, repros) OR (b) an offline test of the function itself by its inputs and outputs: run the original exe code (e.g. under an x86 emulator such as Unicorn on the TS3W.exe bytes, or in-process on recorded inputs) against our model or replacement, with measured basic-block coverage of the function (target: every block reached, plus edge cases) and differential output checks; pure or near-pure functions (hashing, RefPack, lot transform math, lookups, parsers) fit (b). Anything else is carried as a stated risk (Rodney, 2026-10-16). Open now: ledger rows for docs/52-62 (UI drawing, shaders, strings, input, Mono state/GC/caches, autonomy, routing closures, events, IC safety) are not added yet; the next verify build covers them (e.g. the 6 Mono warm-up counters from docs/58, worker-side `vfRt*` from docs/60 §2, IC safety spot checks of thread use).

**Emulator harness (tools/emu, docs/63, 2026-10-16):** `python tools/emu/run.py --self-check` passes on master: `HashStringFNV64_W 0x7CFE40` 312/312, `Lot_WorldToLocal2D 0xAB8540` 438/438 bit-exact (100% blocks), RefPack decode `0x4EB3B0` 212/212 (66/66 blocks). All three touch no `.data` globals for the tested inputs (pure; RefPack safe on any thread). For fix #11 (per-lot sin/cos cache): the cached sin/cos must be rounded to float32 to stay bit-exact; the `-s` term comes from the `-0.0` constant at `0xF988F0`. RefPack returns the header size even on a short stream and 0 for an empty resource (0 is not an error signal). Needs `pip install unicorn capstone`.

**ThreadedObjects stalls (docs/64, 2026-10-16, from existing samples):** slices over 200 ms are 80% single native route searches (`Route_Plan`), run by Sims doing travel interactions (go home, visit lot/community lot, rabbit holes, Go Here With); the 885 ms max is one 862 ms plan. Gangway re-plans (House Boat Fix + game exit path) are 33-38% of plan samples in long slices. Autonomy and SimUpdate are steady per-tick cost, not stalls. Next: cap only retried plans (DoRoute replans, plans to an already-failed lot), skip/reuse gangway re-plans (behaviour spec, no code copy), yield between plans in the ts3fixes `Route.Plan` hook; confirm with a per-task timer at the ThreadedObjects calls `0xD7FA74`/`0xD7FB53` (task id `host+0xBD0`). H6 async routing is the structural fix.

**Lot scoring (docs/65, 2026-10-16):** not a live stutter source: 15-30 µs per call while playing; the 30-116 ms calls are at world load (forced main-view draw `0x6F3C90` via `0xC69FF0` when `Renderer+0x29D` is clear). S3SS Throttle Lot LoD Transitions picks the cheaper branch (`[0x11ECBC0]=1` does the same without S3SS); S3SS also chunks world-load object adds via its `0xAC1130` detour (up to 34 chunks per pump pass, pump max 60 ms). Moving scoring to a worker rejected. `[LotStream]` lot-add µs is not valid with S3SS on (its detour bypasses our timer).

**Crash triage (docs/67, 2026-10-16):** crash A (2026-10-14 11:58, write to `0x694690`) is most likely our own `[Alloc]` cache: `EaFreeHook` caches a pointer freed twice twice, the bin links to itself and two owners share one chunk (bug verified in alloc.cpp; link to the crash inferred; `0x694690` is a device callback, no mod patches it, `0x0804xxxx` is executable game heap, not a mod stub). Keep `[Alloc] Enabled=0`; fix = drop duplicates in the free hook (walk the bin, max 32, count them) and treat count 0 as empty; confirm with an offline double-free selftest, then the alloc soaks. **The lane-G `bp-alloc-soak-1/2`, `ip-alloc-soak-3` jobs (the only lane-G jobs left) are taken out of the resume command until that fix is built.** Crash B (2026-10-15 15:38, `0x5ceb9a`): CAS model builder part teardown on main calls through a float (5.0) as a pointer: game-side lifetime race with ThumbnailManager / sim-thread CAS calls (inferred, fits the docs/43 thumbnail race); no mod or our code on the path; keep `travel_university` without `dialog: true`.

**Hook conflict map (docs/68, 2026-10-16):** S3SS and S3PP patch from their own threads after ts3native loads, so a shared site means ours installs and theirs silently fails. High: `[Verify]` Costs hook on `0xD7F8B0` disables S3PP's stage profiler and with it its type-lookup speed-up (verify timings are not comparable with S3PP on); planned fix #10 at `0xD81FC3` sits inside the bytes S3SS searches for its 480 TPS/60 FPS limiter: put the pump deadline in the existing `RunJobs` hook instead; fix #29 at `0x59C5B0` = S3PP text-lookup cache (pick one). Medium: MonoThread `GC_add_roots 0xE4D9F0` vs S3PP; `ObjScene`/Verify on `0xABFAC0`/`0x7D2DB0` vs S3SS lot streaming (check the S3SS log once). Rule for new hooks: never write inside bytes another mod searches for; `node tools/hook-sites.ts --check` keeps docs/68 complete.

**Decision (Rodney, 2026-10-16): prefer our fixes; no S3PP fork.** S3PP (GPLv3, sonja92yu) switches each feature in its ini, so where one of our fixes overlaps an S3PP feature and wins its A/B, the installer turns that S3PP feature off (queue field `s3ppIni`) and ships ours; S3PP features we do not replace stay on. Inventory done (docs/69): 9 S3PP 2.1 features; keep 4 (`MonoTypeEHashProbe`, `SimulatorStageProfiler`, `MonoIsinstCache`, `PackageIndexWarmup`), compatible 2 (`StblBucketLayoutTuning`, `AllocatorArenaTuning`), replace 1 (`LocalizedStringCache` by #29 after A/B: set `LocalizedStringCache=0`, also needed by the MonoThread `GC_add_roots` hook), off 2 (`ThreadStackDiet` until the docs/66 #2 A/B, `ShortWaitSpin` overlaps S3SS AdaptiveWait). #29 must read the active STBL layout at run time (S3PP shrinks tables from 7,919 to 509 buckets). Installer needs a conditional ini override (`configWhen`) when an overlapping fix ships off by default. A/B jobs A-E listed in docs/69 (profile jobs wait for the sampler-hang fix).

**s3pp-cache queue 2026-10-16: both jobs FAIL, but the game did not hang** (docs/data/results/2026-10-16-sampler-hang.md): every `/simstack` call has timed out since 2026-10-13 10:27 (all `simstack.jsonl` since then are empty, incl. the passing mainstack run); timed-out calls kept running in the agent and starved the watchdog heartbeat, so the queue killed a healthy game. Fixed (agent.js: sim thread id from `[0x11D8A8C]`, module list cached; ts3probe.py: one stack call at a time; profile-world.ts: counts lost samples). Rerun s3pp-cache to confirm; the first run must show Simulate frames in the samples.
